Antes do render, o gate é conferido por `ls` (beat órfão = recusa). Custo-alvo do kit: **0 mco** (screenshots = captura · cutout = matte self-host u2net + sticker-white · data-viz = HTML/SVG da casa); peça que exigir geração paga = 20 mco/peça, declarado antes.

Procedências (Libsyn/AdvertiseCast · IAB 2024) são carimbo de fonte no HUD — nunca texto de cena meu.

Hierarquia entregue ao Encenador: o pico de cada cena é o beat final de `contraria`/`resolve` (3A capa na prateleira nova · 3B carimbo do US$ 625 · 3C selo 2×).

> 215	- **Números — lacunas:** nenhuma. A dramaturgia deste bloco não pediu número que o roteiro não tenha. O US$ 2 bi+ (ancorado, linha 63 do roteiro) só entra COM a expansão aprovada; sem ela, fica fora de voz e de tela.

> 231	## STAGING (VOCAB-STAGING-MONTAR v1) — Encenador · 2026-08-11
> 232	
> 233	**ORO:** Operator: Encenador · Reviewer: DoP + Diretor de Arte (executabilidade) e Sovereign (parecer ocular) · Owner: Sovereign.
> 234	
> 235	**Base material (Lei 1 — molde RELIDO nesta sessão, não por memória):** `scripts/motion/layered/build-scene.mjs` — layout **1280×720** CSS px (master 1080p via dsf 1.5) · `PERSP = 1300` (`:22`) · tabela Z (`:43-51`) · SLAB 460×900, gapX 330 (`:53`) · entrada por folha `translateZ −300→0` + `win()` (`:341-342`, `:278`) · spring (`:275`) · badge carimba in-stage (`:346-347`) · chips desce-spring (`:363-369`) · dado surge-fade (`:373`) · HUD fora do `#stage` (`:157-176`) · scrim (`:159-162`). Projeção de plano em Z: `s = P/(P − (camZ(t) + z))`, centro do quadro (640, 360); caixa projetada de um ponto de cena `(x,y)` = `(640 + x·s, 360 + y·s)`. Toda aritmética abaixo usa isso.

> 237	**Dependência declarada:** `planes{id:z}` é honrado **só pelo runner** (❌ nó — FR-SPACES-164). Se alguém tentar renderizar este bloco pelo nó, o staging inteiro não é honrado → **recuso o render**.

> 833	**≥ 250 × s(fade-start) × 1,5 device px** de largura nativa (com s(fade-start) ≈ 2,0 estimado ⇒

> 842	
> 843	### Regra de encaixe (G-ART-MONTAR-2 aplicado às caixas do Encenador)
> 844	
> 845	As caixas `w×h` do staging são **ENVELOPE de ocupação (contain), nunca molde de stretch**: cada camada raster declara UMA dimensão e a outra sai da razão intrínseca do arquivo. Caso concreto: `cut_tela_corte` tem caixa 280×460, mas um 9:16 real é 280×497,8 — o asset entra a **259×460 dentro da caixa**, com ar. Esticar para preencher = distorção = o vetor que o gate existe para matar. (Ressalva R-A2 — contrato, não conflito.)
> 846	
> 847	### Peso ótico por plano — a lei do MATERIAL (não a da tela)
> 848	
> 849	Na v6 a regra era traço percebido constante no quadro (spread ≤10%). **Em MONTAR 3D a regra muda, e a mudança é física:** o contorno branco está NO papel — papel mais perto = borda aparente mais grossa. Contorno percebido constante entre profundidades diferentes leria como "tudo colado no vidro" e mataria a profundidade que o parallax constrói.
> 850	
> 851	**Lei do bloco:** razão traço/largura **NATIVA constante = 3%** em todas as folhas matted (mesmo papel, mesma tesoura); a variação percebida vem SÓ da projeção `s = P/(P−eff)` — profundidade honesta. O PROIBIDO é razão nativa diferente entre folhas sem motivo declarado.
> 852	
> 853	Traço percebido no instante focal de cada folha (largura projetada = medição do DoP × 3%):
> 854	
> 855	| Folha | w proj. focal (DoP) | traço percebido (CSS px) |
> 856	|---|---|---|
> 857	| cut_pauta | 132 | 4,0 |

> 865	**Piso: 2,5 px CSS na folha mais funda no seu instante focal** (pauta 4,0 ✓ — margem 1,6×) — abaixo disso a assinatura morre nas duas compressões (crf 18 + crf 19). A progressão 4,0→8,3 acompanha exatamente a progressão de escala que o DoP declarou como argumento ("o custo cresce a olho nu") — o contorno engrossa JUNTO: material e dramaturgia dizem a mesma coisa. `--stroke N` nativo por peça = `0,03 × largura útil nativa` (ex.: captura de 800 px → stroke 24). O `strokePx` mínimo de 4 nativos (A43) fica sempre satisfeito; `semiPx` de cada matte vai ao manifesto — **a franja é encoberta DECLARADA, nunca escondida** (G-ART-MONTAR-4; o contorno é assinatura aprovada A43 §19 D1, não curativo envergonhado).

> 24	 *   1. preserve-3d flattening: num elemento com transform-style:preserve-3d, opacity<1,
> 25	 *      filter!=none OU so o ANUNCIO deles em will-change rebaixa o transform-style USADO para
> 26	 *      flat e o rotateY dos filhos vira encolhimento horizontal. Por isso .pl tem
> 27	 *      will-change:transform e NADA MAIS, e o runtime poe opacity/filter SEMPRE na FOLHA
> 28	 *      (o wrapper interno da camada), nunca no plano.
> 29	 *   2. margin negativo + translate = deslocamento DOBRADO. Centragem = translate(-50%,-50%)
> 30	 *      unico; posicao da folha = left/top relativos ao centro do plano.
> 31	 *   3. Crase em comentario dentro do template literal quebra o parse (mordeu 3x no
> 32	 *      scene-template.ts). Nenhum comentario aqui usa crase.
> 33	 *   4. Video local via file:// exige o prefixo explicito (sem ele o <video> falha em silencio,
> 34	 *      readyState 0, duration NaN).
> 35	 *
> 36	 * FORA DO v2.0 (Pedido de Vocabulario se a trupe precisar): wires (fios SVG animados do spike).
> 37	 * 9:16 e ACEITO no spec mas NUNCA foi provado nesta linguagem (OTD-SPACES-058) — o runner avisa.
> 38	 *
> 39	 * Kinds de camada: slab · cutout · chip · badge · data · splitbar · timeline · counter · stamp
> 40	 * O recorte com contorno branco (edge_treatment sticker-white) e ASSINATURA APROVADA do canal
> 41	 * (A43 §19 D1, Sovereign 2026-08-11) — o cutout espera o PNG ja recortado pelo matte.py --stroke.
> 42	 */
> 43	import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "fs";
> 44	
> 45	const [, , specPath, outPath] = process.argv;
> 46	if (!specPath || !outPath) {
> 47	  console.error("uso: montar-template-v2.mjs <spec.json> <out.html>");
> 48	  process.exit(2);
> 49	}
> 50	const spec = JSON.parse(readFileSync(specPath, "utf8"));
> 51	
> 52	const isPortrait = spec.aspect === "9:16";
> 53	if (isPortrait) console.warn("AVISO: 9:16 nunca foi provado na linguagem MONTAR (OTD-SPACES-058) — gate ocular obrigatorio.");
> 54	const W = isPortrait ? 720 : 1280;
> 55	const H = isPortrait ? 1280 : 720;
> 56	const D = Number(spec.duration ?? 8.0);

> 59

> 63	const DRIFT_X = spec.camera?.driftX ?? 24;
> 64	const DRIFT_Y = spec.camera?.driftY ?? 13;
> 65	
> 66	const PLANES = spec.planes ?? {};

> 68	
> 69	const FOCUS = {
> 70	  stations: spec.focus?.stations ?? [[0, Object.keys(PLANES)[0]]],
> 71	  k: spec.focus?.k ?? 80,

> 73	};
> 74	for (const [, pid] of FOCUS.stations) {

> 76	}
> 77	
> 78	const LAYERS = spec.layers ?? [];

> 81	}

> 86	  "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/hyperframes/templates/fonts/Montserrat-variable.ttf",
> 87	).toString("base64");
> 88	
> 89	const esc = (s) => String(s ?? "")
> 90	  .replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
> 91	  .replace(/"/g, "&quot;").replace(/'/g, "&#39;");

> 97	function layerHtml(l, i) {
> 98	  const x = num(l.x), y = num(l.y), w = num(l.w, 300), h = num(l.h, 200);
> 99	  const pos = `left:${x - w / 2}px;top:${y - h / 2}px;width:${w}px;height:${h}px`;
> 100	  switch (l.kind) {
> 101	    case "slab": {
> 102	      const isVideo = l.src?.endsWith(".mp4") || l.src?.endsWith(".webm") || l.media === "video";
> 103	      const media = isVideo
> 104	        ? `<video src="${esc(srcUrl(l.src))}" muted playsinline preload="auto" data-vid="${i}"></video>`
> 105	        : `<img src="${esc(srcUrl(l.src))}" alt="">`;
> 106	      return `<div class="slab" data-leaf="${i}" style="${pos}">
> 107	        ${media}<div class="sheen"></div><div class="rim"></div>
> 108	        ${l.tag ? `<div class="tag">${esc(l.tag)}</div>` : ""}</div>`;
> 109	    }
> 110	    case "cutout":
> 111	      return `<div class="cutwrap" data-leaf="${i}" style="left:${x}px;top:${y}px">
> 112	        <img src="${esc(srcUrl(l.src))}" alt="" style="width:${w}px;height:auto"></div>`;
> 113	    case "chip":
> 114	      return `<div class="chip" data-leaf="${i}" style="left:${x}px;top:${y}px">
> 115	        ${l.iconPath ? `<div class="mk"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#06121f" stroke-width="2.5"><path d="${esc(l.iconPath)}"/></svg></div>` : ""}
> 116	        <div>${l.t1 ? `<div class="t1">${esc(l.t1)}</div>` : ""}${l.t2 ? `<div class="t2">${esc(l.t2)}</div>` : ""}</div></div>`;
> 117	    case "badge":
> 118	      return `<div class="badge" data-leaf="${i}" style="left:${x}px;top:${y}px">
> 119	        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24">${l.iconSvg ?? '<rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="14" rx="2"/><path d="M3 15l5-5 4 4 3-3 6 6"/>'}</svg></div>`;
> 120	    case "data": {
> 121	      const cols = (l.series ?? []).map((s, bi) => `

> 127	      return `<div class="dataviz" data-leaf="${i}" style="left:${x - w / 2}px;top:${y - h / 2}px;width:${w}px">

> 131	    }
> 132	    case "splitbar":

> 134	        ${l.title ? `<div class="dttl">${esc(l.title)}</div>` : ""}

> 136	          <div class="sbL" data-sb="${i}:L"><span class="sbv">${esc(l.left?.label ?? "")}</span><span class="sbk">${esc(l.left?.name ?? "")}</span></div>
> 137	          <div class="sbgap" data-sb="${i}:G"></div>
> 138	          <div class="sbR" data-sb="${i}:R"><span class="sbv">${esc(l.right?.label ?? "")}</span><span class="sbk">${esc(l.right?.name ?? "")}</span></div>
> 139	        </div>
> 140	        ${l.caption ? `<div class="dcap">${esc(l.caption)}</div>` : ""}</div>`;
> 141	    case "timeline": {

> 152	    }
> 153	    case "counter":

## 🤖 Claude *(01:44:34)*

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

I've identified the stamp element in the v2 motor—it's a plane layer with specific typography styling (46px white text and 13px caps with letter-spacing) that functions as a data plate with its own input/output windows and spring behavior by default.

This resolves a discrepancy flagged in the macro documentation, though I need to note this as a supersede-candidate rather than overwriting the existing contract notes, since the v2 motor's native stamp implementation matches the fallback behavior described in the original spec—it sits as a stage layer subject to depth-of-field blur and camera parallax, exactly as intended.

The `data` kind for dataviz uses a structured set of properties: `label` for the displayed value text, `name` for the identifier, `value` for numeric height, plus styling attributes like `fill`, `glow`, and `color`; it also includes `title` and `caption` fields with their own typography rules, where the caption serves as the on-screen source attribution.

Bars normalize against a `max` value and configurable `barMax` (defaulting to 230), with height calculated from zero upward, and each series has its own `grow` window that controls per-bar timing—this is the beat-equivalent capability now alive in v2, though I likely won't need bars for ato3.

The `counter` kind animates from one number to another over a specified window with formatted output in Portuguese, using a 92px gradient text style that resolves the counter implementation requirement, but there's a critical issue: the numerals aren't set to tabular spacing, which means an animated counter will jitter as the proportional glyphs shift width—this violates the principle that any animating number must use tabular numerals, so I need to flag this as a small fix for the ART layer.

For ato3's counter specifically, I'm weighing whether to animate the arithmetic result (25.000 × US$25 = US$625), though the Dramaturgo's specifications might already determine this.  The v2 counter kind could partially address PV-2's request for progressive equation writing—the number could count up from 0 to 625 during the "dá cerca de" moment rather than just appearing as a static stamp. I should note this as a potential upgrade path in the contract notes, but the final call belongs to the Dramaturgo and DoP.

However, there are timing and precision concerns: an animated counter with non-tabular numerals might jitter, and the counter lacks a source line. More importantly, the DoP already placed a `carimba-spring` at frame 27 with specific timing that conflicts with a 0.6–2.6 second counting window. I'll keep the counter as a declared option but preserve the `stamp` approach as the primary solution—it's cleaner since the stamp kind supports both text ("≈ US$ 625") and source attribution ("PREMISSA: 25.000 × US$ 25"), which is exactly what `eq_resultado_viz` needs.

Now I'm reviewing the other component options: `splitbar` for left/right splits (not needed here), `chip` for small labeled notification-like items, and `slab` for image or video containers with styling like border-radius, purple glow effects, and critical `object-fit: cover` for proper image scaling.

The key insight is that `object-fit: cover` crops the image to fill the declared width and height without distortion—it preserves aspect ratio while cropping overflow. So when I declare both `w` and `h` on a slab, I'm creating a crop window rather than stretching the content, which is safe from distortion but requires intentional crop declaration.

In contrast, `cutout` uses `width:${w}px;height:auto`, which means only the width matters and the height adjusts automatically to preserve the intrinsic aspect ratio. I can now state this precisely: cutouts declare `w` only (height is ignored and set to auto), while slabs declare both w and h and crop via cover.

I'm also noticing that `.cutwrap img` has a hardcoded drop-shadow effect with a cyan glow applied...

Cyan glow on cutouts...

the drop-shadow traces the alpha silhouette, which works well for sticker effects. Now I'm mapping out the HUD elements — the kick, rule, title, and caption positioning with their light weights, plus the end window and scrim layers that all fade out based on the hudOut multiplier, which defaults to a range calculated from the document height. The caption disappears at hudOut in v2, so that's another constraint for the VOC-ART-M specification.

The real solution for the caption problem is that the `stamp` layer handles it in-stage, and the data caption lives inside the dataviz layer with its own opacity window tied to the data layer's lifecycle, not the HUD's. This means the chart carries its own caption that fades with the chart itself, which solves the G4 requirement — the caption element sits inside the dataviz div and respects the layer's opacity timing.

Now I'm seeing that both VOC-ART-004 and VOC-ART-M-002 are actually resolved by the v2 motor through the `caption` property on data layers and the `source` property on stamp layers, which is a material win that should be documented as superseding the pending request in macro.md. I'm also noting the fixed typography scales available in v2: the dataviz title uses 16px bold with letter spacing, bar value labels are 44px bold with a 15px caps variant, and the data caption is set at 14.5px with tracking.

Adding more typography scales... Stamp, chip, badge, and counter elements are positioned at their top-left corner (x,y) without centering transforms, whereas slab, data, splitbar, and timeline use centered positioning with offset calculations. I need to clarify this coordinate system distinction to the Encenador since their original coordinates were written with centering in mind. `__fatal` on…

</details>

## 🤖 Claude *(01:54:18)*

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

I'm moving forward with the M-series scope for my lens, specifically continuing from VOC-ART-M-003 onward for new requests. Now I'm planning the ARTE section for ato3-audio.md and reviewing the material findings from this session, including the production motor v2 script that's about 30KB with over 500 lines.

The motor enforces a closed vocabulary of component kinds (slab, cutout, chip, badge, data, splitbar, timeline, counter, stamp) with strict validation that exits on unknown types. It loads Montserrat as a variable font from base64, and the stamp component is particularly notable—it's a 46px white element with a 13px caps label in light blue, featuring its own in/out animations and spring behavior by default. This directly addresses the fallback from the earlier M-002 request without overriding their original ask. The data component uses a structured layout with title, labeled columns, and captions positioned within the sheet itself at 14.5px in a muted blue tone.

Bar normalization follows a simple formula where height equals the bar maximum times the value divided by the series maximum, with zero as the baseline. The real risk is when the ratio between minimum and maximum values is too high (≥0.8), which makes all bars appear nearly identical. For the counter component, I'm using Intl.NumberFormat for Portuguese localization with a 92px animated number that applies a white-to-cyan gradient via background clipping. However, there's a critical issue: the numerals aren't tabular, which causes digit jitter during animation—this violates the requirement in section 4.1, so I'm flagging this as a new constraint violation.

For the cutout feature, the image width is set dynamically while height scales automatically to preserve the intrinsic aspect ratio. The cutout wrapper applies a cyan glow shadow effect across the entire image, which means third-party logos can never be used as cutouts since the glow treatment would violate brand guidelines.

For the slab variant, the image uses object-fit cover to crop the content within the specified dimensions without distortion, and it's wrapped with a purple-tinted border, a diagonal white sheen overlay, an inset violet glow, and matching box shadow effects — so third-party screenshots get the house styling applied automatically. the white overlay sits heaviest in the top-left quadrant, so I need to reposition the screenshot crop to keep the Spotify wordmark clear of that bright zone. Let me map out the gradient angle — at 102 degrees, the intensity peaks along the upper-left edge and fades toward the bottom-right, which means I should shift the content downward or rightward to avoid the mark sitting under that gloss. avoidable, I'll route the mark into the plaque/chip instead. On the image opacity front, there's a global 95% opacity applied to screenshots within dark slabs, which creates a subtle 5% darkening effect across the entire capture—not a selective recolor, but a container property I need to declare honestly as a treatment that warrants the owner's review.

For the Spotify mark itself, I'm taking the conservative approach: it only appears as natural UI within editorial screenshots, never as a standalone vector logo. Even in those screenshots it carries the mold's opacity and sheen, so I'm declaring that and offering the text-only fallback as the safe default, with the official logo available only if the owner approves. This aligns with the two-tier strategy of plain text first, then gated access to the official mark.

Now I'm working through the HUD elements—the kick, rule, title, and cap components all scale through a multiplier function with a default window calculation...

Adjusting the cap timing in the HUD to fire earlier, which mirrors an issue I flagged before. The font stamp can't live in the HUD itself; it needs to go on the sheet as part of the caption data instead. This resolves one of my mandates and gives another approach a native executable path.

For the image guard function, a broken asset now aborts before the first frame even renders, which ties into my beat-building requirement...

the engine v2 already handles orphaned layer references that would otherwise leave frame gaps. I'm noting the positioning anchor mismatch: most elements like slabs and timelines center on their coordinates, but cutouts, chips, badges, counters, and stamps anchor from the top-left corner instead. The director wrote everything as center points, so either the engine or the coordinate system needs adjustment.

Since stamps have intrinsic width from their nowrap styling and padding, converting their positions requires measuring them at runtime. I'm also checking that the focus.stations interpolation is cumulative—each station smoothly transitions over 0.8 seconds starting at its own time. And I notice the brand-catalog motion script doesn't actually exist in the codebase.

For the art direction in Act 3, I'm laying out the dramaturgical layers: the audio foundation with waveform visualization, the Spotify UI panel in portrait orientation, and the episode artwork as a sticker element.

Then for layer 3B, I'm adding the CPM track visualization, the EQ premise, factor, and result visualizations stacked vertically, with a contingent market visualization option. Layer 3C brings in the episode video card, the cut artwork, and the seal element positioned in the HUD area with a fallback stage placement.

The HUD itself contains the kicker and source stamps positioned on the left and right sides across sections 3A through 3C.

Now I'm thinking through how to map these elements to the v2 system. The waveform visualization is tricky — it's technically bars, but using a data-viz form for something decorative would be misleading. The waveform is really an image representation of the audio itself, so it should be treated as a cutout PNG with alpha transparency, generated directly from the narration audio using ffmpeg.

The cyan glow that comes with cutout styling actually works well here — it reads as an emissive signal and reinforces that this is our own authored piece. Since we're generating the waveform from our own narration, there's no attribution needed, and we can skip the white stroke entirely by not applying it at the file level. So the waveform becomes a cutout kind, rendered as a PNG with alpha from the EP07 narration audio itself. For the Spotify UI screenshot, I'm weighing a two-step decision similar to how I handled the Adsense mark in act 2: either use a real mobile screenshot showing a Brazilian podcast page (which satisfies nominative use since Spotify is mentioned in the narration itself), or find an alternative that minimizes legal risk while keeping the visual reference intact. Adding styling details... Masking UI metrics and third-party podcast names from the crop to avoid monetization issues — the shelf needs to display episodes, but showing another creator's podcast name in a paid video creates a conflict, so I'm considering whether we should use our own podcast content instead, though that's not currently available. The cleanest approach is to capture our own Spotify podcast page—if we create the show through Spotify for Podcasters, the screenshot becomes entirely ours with Spotify's interface intact and our own content, which sidesteps third-party copyright issues entirely. This mirrors the same principle we'd apply to a TikTok screenshot of our own account running our own content, keeping the doctrine consistent across platforms.

For the empty slot the Encenador mentioned, P1 solves it authentically—it's a real gap in our own list, not fabricated. With P2, any empty space must appear naturally in the capture; we never delete an item just to create the void, since that crosses into doctoring evidence, which violates the precedent from act 2 about invisible pixel edits being falsification. P3 falls back to plain text typography if needed, losing the visual recognition but preserving the information.

Now I'm working with the cutout piece from our own EP07 cover art—this closes out the pending manifesto macro.md flagged. The matte removal uses the u2net model with specific parameters, but since the cover is a perfect rectangle (1:1 square artwork), I don't actually need the matte removal; a clean white border handles it, following the precedent that perfect rectangles skip the matting step.

So I'm setting the edge to use a white geometric sticker frame drawn directly into the file itself, with no model processing and zero fringing—simpler and cheaper. The rectangular cutout accepts any PNG, and the white frame is baked into the artwork. For the optical weight, I'm compensating the stroke based on each station's projection: the cover scales to about 167×167 at k 0.929 for the 3A position, and 270×270 CSS for 3C at the same scale factor.

Now I'm facing a choice between two conflicting approaches. I could export two separate versions with different stroke widths for each station, or use a single export with a constant native stroke-to-width ratio of 3% that scales with perspective. The documentation has competing precedents—estrutura.md uses the material law (constant native ratio, perceived width varies with depth), while ato1 and macro prefer perceived consistency at the focal station with a second export only when needed. I need to decide which principle applies when the same asset is used across different stations.

Let me work through the math to see if a single file with constant ratio actually works. If I set the native ratio so that the perceived stroke is 4 pixels at station 3A (where the projected width is 167), that gives me a ratio of about 2.4%. Scaling that same ratio to station 3C with its projected width of 251 would yield a perceived stroke of 6 pixels—which breaks the "≤10% spread" constraint but might be acceptable depending on the visual context.

With two separate exports, I could tune each station independently: 2.4% for 3A and 1.59% for 3C, both landing at exactly 4 pixels. But the real question is whether this matters. My own principle states that perceived stroke should remain constant within a frame, and since each frame contains only one stroked element, there's no internal spread to violate. The constraint about spread applies across elements within the same system, not across different frames. So the choice really comes down to whether the object needs to read as visually continuous across the different cuts.

The honest approach is to use a constant native ratio—it's the same physical piece, just appearing thicker as it comes closer to the viewer. With a constant 2.4%, I'd get 4 pixels for 3A and 6 pixels for 3C, both well above the minimum threshold of 2.5 pixels, and the growth feels coherent with the piece moving closer. Plus, in 3C where the video card sits beside the capa, if that card also has a white border, the spreads need to match. Looking at what the video card actually is—a screenshot of the player showing the same cover art—it's a slab with a mold rim and sheen, so the borders should align.

But here's the problem: if the video card is a slab, it wouldn't have a white sticker border, which means in 3C the two twins would have different edge treatments—one with white, one without. That breaks the whole visual relationship; they wouldn't read as twins anymore. So the decision is clear: both twins in 3C need the same edge treatment, either both with the sticker-white border or neither. Since the cover is already reused from 3A with its border, the video card has to be treated as a cutout with the same sticker-white geometric border at the matched native ratio.

This protects the photometric twinning the DoP demanded—a slab's violet rim and gloss on one twin but not the other would destroy that light-reading consistency. I'm essentially co-signing the DoP's photometry through the material choice.

Now checking the perceived stroke widths: the card projects to 446 pixels wide while the cover projects to 251, creating a 1.78× spread—that's 78% difference, which violates the G7 constraint of staying under 10%.

Since both twins appear in the same frame at the same depth in 3C, I need to apply the perceived constant rule instead. Working backward from a target perceived width of 4.5 pixels, the card needs a 1.01% native ratio while the cover needs 1.79%—but then in 3A the cover alone requires 2.40%, meaning the cover needs two separate exports at different ratios depending on the shot.

For the actual pixel dimensions, I'm setting the native file width to 1024 pixels (a reasonable size for cover art). In 3A with CSS width 180 and the scale factor around 0.929, that translates to 251 device pixels, which multiplied by 1.5 gives me the native requirement. In 3C with CSS 270, I get 376 device pixels—both comfortably within the 1024 native width. Converting the stroke percentages to actual pixels: the 3A export needs 25 pixels of stroke, while 3C needs about 18 pixels.

For the card element at 1280 native pixels (16:9 aspect ratio), the CSS width of 480 with the same scale factor requires a minimum of 669 device pixels, and the stroke works out to 13 pixels. When I check the perceived stroke widths across all three pieces—the 3A cover at 4.08 pixels, the 3C cover at 4.41 pixels, and the card at 4.53 pixels—the variation in 3C is only 2.7%, well under the 10% tolerance I'm aiming for.

Nada aqui \u00e9\naconselhamento jur\u00eddico; publicar a primeira l\u00e2mina com marca alheia \u00e9 decis\u00e3o do Owner.\n\n### A3-0 \u00b7 Base material (Lei 1 \u2014 verificada NESTA sess\u00e3o, nada por mem\u00f3ria)\n\n\u26a0\ufe0f **As tr\u00eas lentes anteriores escreveram contra `scripts/motion/layered/build-scene.mjs`. O motor de\nPRODU\u00c7\u00c3O \u00e9 `scripts/motion/montar-template-v2.mjs`** (30.508 B \u00b7 532 l \u00b7 mtime 2026-08-11 13:19) \u2014 eu\nsou a primeira lente a escrever contra ele. **Conferi constante a constante que a aritm\u00e9tica delas\nsobrevive intacta:** `perspective ?? 1300` (`:57`) \u00b7 `origin ?? \"50% 47%\"` (`:58`) \u00b7 `driftX/Y ?? 24/13`\n(`:63-64`) \u00b7 `focus.k ?? 80`, `maxBlur ?? 11` (`:71-72`) \u00b7 `dof(z)=clamp(|z\u2212fz|/k,0,max)` **por FOLHA**\n(`:409`, `:419`) \u00b7 `camZ` por esta\u00e7\u00f5es com `smooth` (`:343-352`) \u00b7 weave 1680\u00d71000 (`:220`). **Nenhuma\ndesigualdade do Encenador nem esta\u00e7\u00e3o do DoP muda.** O que muda \u00e9 o que \u00e9 meu: os *kinds*.\n\n| Achado no motor v2 | Linha | Consequ\u00eancia para esta lente |\n|---|---|---|\n| **Vocabul\u00e1rio FECHADO no c\u00f3digo**: `slab\u00b7cutout\u00b7chip\u00b7badge\u00b7data\u00b7splitbar\u00b7timeline\u00b7counter\u00b7stamp`; kind desconhecido \u2192 `process.exit(2)` | `:39`, `:100-164`, `:161-163` | token improvisado **n\u00e3o vira frame silencioso \u2014 vira exit 2**. O motor virou meu gate G8 |\n| **`stamp` = carimbo com linha de FONTE embutida**: `.sv` 46px/800 branco + `.ssrc` 13px caps track .16em `#9fd8ff`, camada de PALCO com `in`/`out` pr\u00f3prios e `spring` por default | `:157-160`, `:295-300`, `:185`, `:187`, `:433` | **G4 executa hoje**: dado e proced\u00eancia na MESMA pe\u00e7a, com a MESMA vida. Resolve na pr\u00e1tica o **VOC-ART-004** do meu mandato para esta linguagem |\n| **`data.caption` (`.dcap` 14,5px `#93aac4`) vive DENTRO da folha** \u2014 opacidade da camada, n\u00e3o do HUD | `:130`, `:274`, `:417-418` | idem para gr\u00e1ficos (n\u00e3o usado aqui \u2014 ver A3-3) |\n| **HUD `#kick`/`#rule`/`#title`/`#cap` multiplicados por `hudOut`** (janela default `[min(3, D\u00b70,45), min(3,8, D\u00b70,55)]`) | `:481-488` | **carimbo de fonte no HUD morre cedo** \u2014 mesma armadilha do `build-scene.mjs:395`. Por isso **n\u00e3o uso** as faixas `sourceStamp-*` do Encenador |\n| **`cutout` = `width:${w}px; height:auto`** | `:112` | raz\u00e3o intr\u00ednseca preservada por constru\u00e7\u00e3o; **s\u00f3 `w` conta** (G-ART-MONTAR-2 satisfeito pelo motor) |\n| **`.cutwrap img` imp\u00f5e `drop-shadow(0 0 30px rgba(0,215,255,.30))`** \u2014 glow ciano em TODO recorte | `:247-249` | \u00e9 o `.icon-disc` do v6 reencarnado: **marca de terceiro NUNCA entra como `cutout`** (halo sobre a silhueta do logo = G2) |\n| **`slab`**: `object-fit:cover` + `opacity:.95` na imagem \u00b7 `.sheen` (v\u00e9u branco .20\u2192.03) \u00b7 `.rim` violeta \u00b7 borda 1px violeta \u00b7 `border-radius:32px` | `:236-246`, `:240-242` | a caixa \u00e9 **janela de CROP** (zero distor\u00e7\u00e3o) \u2014 e o container **veste** a captura: gate de enquadramento da marca em A3-2 e pedido **VOC-ART-M-004** |\n| **`counter`** from\u2192to, `Intl.NumberFormat('pt-BR')`, `.cv` 92px/800 | `:153-156`, `:292-293`, `:471-478` | **PV-1 do Dramaturgo est\u00e1 materialmente ATENDIDO no v2** (registro, n\u00e3o \u00e9 pedido meu) \u2014 com a ressalva do item seguinte |\n| **`grep -c \"tabular\"` = 0** no motor inteiro | \u2014 | n\u00famero que **anima** sai com algarismo proporcional \u21d2 **treme frame a frame**. N\u00e3o morde este bloco (todos os n\u00fameros s\u00e3o est\u00e1ticos); morde o dia em que o `counter` entrar \u2192 **VOC-ART-M-005** |\n| **`guardImages` \u2192 `window.__fatal`** antes do frame 1 quando um `<img>` falha | `:503-518` | **meu G-ART-MONTAR-6 est\u00e1 FECHADO no v2**: asset \u00f3rf\u00e3o n\u00e3o vira mais 300 frames com buraco e `exit 0` |\n| **Ancoragem por kind**: `slab/data/splitbar/timeline` centram (`x \u2212 w/2`); **`cutout/chip/badge/counter/stamp` ancoram pelo CANTO SUPERIOR-ESQUERDO** | `:99`, `:111`, `:114`, `:118`, `:154`, `:158` | o Encenador escreveu tudo como CENTRO \u2192 convers\u00e3o obrigat\u00f3ria (A3-5) |\n| `#wash`/`#weave` **fora do `#stage`**, sem `dof` | `:318-319`, `:401-404` | o `fundo_audio` **n\u00e3o recebe blur** \u2014 o or\u00e7amento de \u00e1rea do DoP (\u00a7F3-7) fica **menor** que o previsto |\n\n**Fora do motor:** `scripts/hyperframes/templates/fonts/Montserrat-variable.ttf` **744.936 B** (`ls`), lida\ne embutida em base64 (`:85-87`, `@font-face 'Mont'` `:210`) \u21d2 **`display-montserrat` EXECUTA** \u00b7\n`matte.py --stroke N` vivo (`:11-13`, `:52-58` \u2014 *\"hides matting fuzz by design\"*) \u00b7 u2net digest\nconferido por `cat` agora: **`8d10d2f3bb75ae3b6d527c77944fc5e7dcd94b29809d47a739a7a728a912b491`**\n(`/home/ubuntu/.mcorch/motion-models/SHA256SUMS`) \u00b7 **`scripts/motion/brand-catalog.ts` N\u00c3O EXISTE**\n(`ls` desta sess\u00e3o) \u2014 VOC-ART-001 segue aberto e **n\u00e3o bloqueia este bloco**, porque a decis\u00e3o-mestre \u00e9\nzero marca vetorial. O que n\u00e3o li est\u00e1 marcado **A CONFERIR** \u2014 nunca afirmado.\n\n**Assinatura visual (A43 \u00a719 D1, selada 2026-08-11):** `edge_treatment: \"sticker-white\"` **APROVADO**.\n**Onde este bloco usa:** `capa_ep_cut` e `ep_video_card` \u2014 os dois em **`sticker-white-geometrico`**\n(ret\u00e2ngulo perfeito dispensa u2net: contorno branco composto no arquivo, **`model: none`, `semiPx` 0,\nfranja ZERO**; declaro a diferen\u00e7a de t\u00e9cnica, n\u00e3o a escondo). **Onde N\u00c3O usa:** `waveform_viz` (\u00e9\nsinal, n\u00e3o mat\u00e9ria recortada \u2014 contorno ali leria como adesivo sobre um som) e a l\u00e2mina `spotify_ui`\n(janela, n\u00e3o pe\u00e7a colada; e o kind `slab` n\u00e3o oferece o contorno). **Em nenhuma marca o contorno toca o\nlogo** \u2014 ele emoldura a CAPTURA inteira, com respiro (G2). **Consequ\u00eancia declarada: este bloco n\u00e3o roda\nu2net em pe\u00e7a nenhuma** \u2014 zero infer\u00eancia, zero franja a encobrir.\n\n### A3-1 \u00b7 Decis\u00f5es-mestre do bloco\n\n1. **A marca entra pelo habitat, nunca extra\u00edda.** Spotify e YouTube aparecem **s\u00f3** como chrome de UI\n   **dentro de captura editorial**, `treatment: intacto`. **Logo vetorial isolado: RECUSADO por mim**\n   (degrau GATED em A3-6) \u2014 a UI j\u00e1 entrega o reconhecimento que o beat pede, e o logo isolado \u00e9\n   exatamente o que consome o fator 2 do uso nominativo (*Toyota v. Tabari*: os r\u00e9us podiam comunicar\n   *\"without using the visual trappings\"*). **Spotify \u00e9 CITADO na narra\u00e7\u00e3o** \u21d2 G3 satisfeito.\n2. **O epis\u00f3dio-testemunha \u00e9 NOSSO e j\u00e1 publicado.** `capa_ep_cut` (3A/3C) e `ep_video_card` (3C) s\u00e3o o\n   **mesmo epis\u00f3dio da casa**, na mesma arte \u2014 \u00e9 o que torna a gemelaridade de 3C **verdadeira** e n\u00e3o\n   encenada, e mata copyright de obra alheia no quadro final do bloco. Depend\u00eancia de produ\u00e7\u00e3o real em\n   A3-6 (Rota A).\n3. **Os g\u00eameos de 3C s\u00e3o da MESMA esp\u00e9cie de pe\u00e7a.** Ambos `cutout` com contorno branco. Um `slab`\n   (borda violeta + `.sheen` + `.rim`) ao lado de um recorte branco seriam **dois materiais**, e a tese\n   \"mesma aten\u00e7\u00e3o\" morre na mat\u00e9ria antes de morrer no argumento \u2014 ver Dissenso **R-A3-1**.\n4. **A conta se declara na pr\u00f3pria pe\u00e7a.** Premissa, fator e resultado carregam a condicionalidade\n   DENTRO do `stamp` (`.sv` = o n\u00famero \u00b7 `.ssrc` = a natureza do n\u00famero). Nenhum n\u00famero deste bloco\n   aparece sem a linha que diz o que ele \u00e9.\n5. **Zero pe\u00e7a gerada por IA \u00b7 zero pe\u00e7a do spike `ada39fae`** (16 assets sem fonte/licen\u00e7a \u2014\n   OTD-SPACES-061; **RECUSO** reus\u00e1-los). Proveni\u00eancia AI Act: n/a por constru\u00e7\u00e3o, declarado.\n\n### A3-2 \u00b7 `art.type` \u2014 tipografia, hierarquia, pigmento\n\n```json\n\"art.type\": {\n  \"family\": \"display-montserrat\",\n  \"familyProva\": \"Montserrat-variable.ttf (744.936 B, OFL) lida e embutida em base64 no @font-face 'Mont' \u2014 montar-template-v2.mjs:85-87 e :210. EXECUTA no rail MONTAR (no N\u00d3 segue VOC-ART-006 aberto; este bloco produz pelo runner)\",\n  \"scales\": {\n    \"dado\":        \".stamp .sv \u2014 46px/800, letter-spacing \u2212.02em (os 4 n\u00fameros do bloco + o selo 2\u00d7)\",\n    \"qualificador\":\".stamp .ssrc \u2014 13px/caps, tracking .16em, #9fd8ff (a NATUREZA do n\u00famero: hip\u00f3tese \u00b7 exemplo \u00b7 fonte \u00b7 aproxima\u00e7\u00e3o)\",\n    \"hud\":         \"#kick 12,5px/700 caps tracking .34em #7fe6ff \u2014 sinal de bloco, n\u00e3o carrega beat\"\n  },\n  \"numerals\": \"estatico-proporcional-ok\",\n  \"numeralsNota\": \"tabular N\u00c3O existe no motor (grep -c tabular = 0). Todos os n\u00fameros deste bloco s\u00e3o EST\u00c1TICOS (stamp com spring, valor fixo) \u21d2 proporcional \u00e9 aceit\u00e1vel. REGRA DE GATILHO declarada: se qualquer n\u00famero passar a ANIMAR (o kind `counter` existe e atende o PV-1), tabular vira obrigat\u00f3rio \u2014 o d\u00edgito proporcional muda de largura a cada valor e o n\u00famero TREME. Pedido: VOC-ART-M-005.\"\n}\n```\n\n**Hierarquia \u2014 DOIS n\u00edveis no palco, e isso \u00e9 decis\u00e3o, n\u00e3o pobreza.** Raz\u00e3o 46/13 = **3,5\u00d7** (alvo\n\u22652,5 \u2713), e a separa\u00e7\u00e3o n\u00e3o \u00e9 s\u00f3 de corpo: **peso** 800 \u00d7 700, **tracking** \u2212.02em \u00d7 +.16em, **pigmento**\nbranco \u00d7 ciano-claro. Cada pe\u00e7a deste bloco carrega **um** dado e **uma** qualifica\u00e7\u00e3o \u2014 um terceiro\nn\u00edvel no palco seria um papel que a cena n\u00e3o tem. O `#kick` (12,5px) \u00e9 irm\u00e3o do n\u00edvel 2, vive no HUD e\nmorre no `hudOut` por constru\u00e7\u00e3o: \u00e9 sinaliza\u00e7\u00e3o, nunca informa\u00e7\u00e3o de beat.\n\n**Pigmento \u2014 a declara\u00e7\u00e3o honesta de um campo que eu N\u00c3O tenho aqui.** Nos kinds que este bloco usa\n(`stamp`, `cutout`, `slab`) **toda cor \u00e9 hardcoded no molde**: `.sv` branco, `.ssrc` `#9fd8ff`, borda do\nstamp violeta (`:295-300`), glow ciano do cutout (`:249`), rim violeta do slab (`:243`), wash\nvioleta/ciano (`:214-219`). O \u00fanico pigmento parametriz\u00e1vel do motor \u00e9 `series[].fill/glow/color` do\nkind `data` \u2014 que este bloco n\u00e3o usa. **Consequ\u00eancia declarada:** o token MIV *\"gold s\u00f3 para VALOR\"*\n**n\u00e3o executa aqui** \u2014 US$ 18\u201327 e US$ 625 saem em branco. N\u00e3o \u00e9 esquecimento nem prefer\u00eancia: \u00e9\naus\u00eancia de campo, e ela est\u00e1 pedida em **VOC-ART-M-005** (com impacto em TODOS os blocos irm\u00e3os, que\nespecificaram d\u00edgitos gold).\n\n### A3-3 \u00b7 `art.layers[]` \u2014 por cena (a CAIXA \u00e9 do Encenador; o que vive DENTRO \u00e9 meu)\n\n#### CENA 3A (`ep07-a3-c01-segunda-vida`)\n\n```json\n\"art\": { \"layers\": [\n  { \"id\": \"waveform_viz\", \"family\": \"dado\", \"kind\": \"cutout\", \"plane\": \"waveform_viz (z \u22122600)\",\n    \"w\": 460, \"edge\": \"none\",\n    \"edgeWhy\": \"n\u00e3o \u00e9 mat\u00e9ria recortada, \u00e9 SINAL \u2014 contorno de adesivo sobre uma forma de onda leria como colagem de um som, que n\u00e3o \u00e9 objeto. Este \u00e9 o gate que importa: G7 mede o **mesmo quadro**.\n- **Continuidade entre cenas:** 4,08 \u2192 4,41 (+8%) \u2014 a pe\u00e7a-testemunha atravessa 3A\u21923C sem trocar de\n  identidade material.\n- **Piso de compress\u00e3o:** 4,08 px \u00e9 o m\u00ednimo do bloco, **1,6\u00d7 acima do piso de 2,5 px** que as duas\n  compress\u00f5es (`crf 18` + `crf 19`) exigem \u2713.\n- **Por que DOIS exports da mesma capa:** um export s\u00f3, com raz\u00e3o nativa constante, entregaria 4,08 em\n  3A e **6,0** em 3C ao lado de um card de 4,53 \u2014 **spread de 32% no MESMO quadro**, exatamente o\n  defeito \"desenhado por tr\u00eas pessoas\" que o G7 existe para matar. Dois exports custam segundos.\n- **Fora da lei:** `waveform_viz` (edge none) e os `stamp` (vetor de texto, sem contorno) \u2014 n/a\n  declarado. A borda de 1px do `slab` projeta \u2248 **0,79 px** na esta\u00e7\u00e3o de 3A: vai **aliasar** nas duas\n  compress\u00f5es \u2014 aceito e declarado, porque quem carrega a leitura de borda da l\u00e2mina \u00e9 o `.rim`\n  (sombra inset de 70px), e a hairline **n\u00e3o carrega informa\u00e7\u00e3o nenhuma**.\n\n### A3-5 \u00b7 Encaixe, ancoragem e resolu\u00e7\u00e3o nativa\n\n1. **Envelope, nunca molde (G-ART-MONTAR-2).** As caixas `w\u00d7h` do Encenador s\u00e3o **ocupa\u00e7\u00e3o m\u00e1xima**.\n   O motor j\u00e1 protege: `cutout` usa `width:w; height:auto` (`:112`) \u2014 a segunda dimens\u00e3o sai da raz\u00e3o\n   intr\u00ednseca, stretch \u00e9 imposs\u00edvel. Onde declarei `w` **e** `h` (`spotify_ui`) \u00e9 porque o `slab` usa\n   `object-fit:cover` (`:240`): a caixa \u00e9 **janela de crop**, n\u00e3o deforma\u00e7\u00e3o \u2014 e o crop est\u00e1 declarado\n   com percentual e gate de captura em A3-3.\n2. **\u26a0\ufe0f Ancoragem por kind \u2014 convers\u00e3o obrigat\u00f3ria antes do render.** `slab/data/splitbar/timeline`\n   ancoram pelo **centro** (`x \u2212 w/2`); **`cutout`, `stamp`, `chip`, `badge` e `counter` ancoram pelo\n   CANTO SUPERIOR-ESQUERDO** (`:111`, `:114`, `:118`, `:154`, `:158`). Todas as coordenadas das tr\u00eas\n   lentes anteriores s\u00e3o **centros**. \u21d2 para as 7 camadas `cutout`/`stamp` deste bloco:\n   `x_spec = x_centro \u2212 w/2` e `y_spec = y_centro \u2212 h/2`. Nos `stamp` a largura \u00e9 **intr\u00ednseca**\n   (`white-space:nowrap` + padding 44/26), ent\u00e3o a convers\u00e3o s\u00f3 fecha com a largura MEDIDA no probe \u2014\n   as estimativas de A3-3 s\u00e3o o ponto de partida, **n\u00e3o a prova**. Sem esta convers\u00e3o, a equa\u00e7\u00e3o de 3B\n   desliza para a direita e a aritm\u00e9tica de colis\u00e3o do Encenador deixa de valer.\n3. **Resolu\u00e7\u00e3o nativa (G-ART-MONTAR-5):** calculada contra o `s_max` real de cada cena, n\u00e3o contra a\n   esta\u00e7\u00e3o focal \u2014 `spotify_ui` 2,0\u00d7 de folga \u00b7 `capa` 2,7\u00d7 \u00b7 `card` 1,9\u00d7 \u00b7 `waveform` gerado a 3\u00d7.\n   Nenhuma pe\u00e7a \u00e9 ampliada al\u00e9m do nativo em nenhum instante da curva do DoP.\n4. **Nitidez de texto sob escala > 1:** `selo_2x` projeta a `s = 1,300` \u2014 o compositor pode rasterizar\n   o glifo antes do transform (texto *soft*). **Item obrigat\u00f3rio de probe** (mesmo sintoma registrado\n   pelo bloco MACRO): se soft, \u00e9 t\u00e9cnica do engineer, n\u00e3o maquiagem de arte.\n\n### A3-6 \u00b7 dataViz \u2014 a conta que declara a pr\u00f3pria premissa\n\n```json\n\"dataViz\": {\n  \"form\": \"carimbo-de-dado (kind `stamp`) \u00d74 \u2014 ZERO gr\u00e1fico neste bloco\",\n  \"porqueSemGrafico\": \"as quatro grandezas n\u00e3o formam s\u00e9rie: uma FAIXA de mercado (18\u201327), uma HIP\u00d3TESE de audi\u00eancia (25.000), um PONTO ESCOLHIDO dentro da faixa (US$ 25) e um RESULTADO DERIVADO (625). Barra lado a lado compararia coisas de naturezas diferentes \u2014 sintaxe de gr\u00e1fico, sem\u00e2ntica falsa (G6). O que a cena pede n\u00e3o \u00e9 compara\u00e7\u00e3o: \u00e9 DERIVA\u00c7\u00c3O, e a forma honesta da deriva\u00e7\u00e3o \u00e9 a linha de carimbos que se acumula no quadro\",\n  \"eixo\": \"N/A \u2014 n\u00e3o h\u00e1 eixo \u21d2 n\u00e3o h\u00e1 eixo a truncar, e isso \u00e9 constru\u00e7\u00e3o, n\u00e3o sorte\",\n  \"empate\": \"N/A \u2014 n\u00e3o h\u00e1 rank \u21d2 nenhum empate a falsear (a regra min/max \u2265 0,8 n\u00e3o tem sobre o que incidir)\",\n  \"intervalo\": \"18\u201327 grafados como INTERVALO no mesmo corpo/peso/pigmento; proibido m\u00e9dia, ponto m\u00e9dio, barra de faixa ou qualquer forma que sugira valor \u00fanico\",\n  \"aritmetica\": \"no instante do carimbo o quadro carrega PREMISSA (SE 25.000 \u00b7 hip\u00f3tese) + FATOR (\u00d7 US$ 25 \u00b7 exemplo na faixa) + RESULTADO (\u2248 US$ 625 \u00b7 conta aproximada). O espectador v\u00ea de onde o n\u00famero saiu \u2014 \u00e9 a exig\u00eancia do roteiro ('declarando a premissa') e o anticorpo da regra-m\u00e3e do EP07\",\n  \"fonteNaTela\": \"Libsyn \u00b7 AdvertiseCast no .ssrc da pr\u00f3pria pe\u00e7a que carrega o CPM, com a MESMA vida da pe\u00e7a (montar-template-v2.mjs:295-300 + :185). Nada depende do HUD, que morre no hudOut\",\n  \"numerals\": \"proporcional (est\u00e1tico) \u2014 gatilho tabular declarado em A3-2\",\n  \"gateDeParada\": \"se, no dia da curadoria, a faixa publicada por Libsyn/AdvertiseCast n\u00e3o for mais 18\u201327, PARA e volta ao Sovereign. N\u00famero de mercado envelhece; ajuste silencioso \u00e9 falsifica\u00e7\u00e3o (precedente ART-2 do Ato 2)\"\n}\n```\n\n### A3-7 \u00b7 KIT A MATERIALIZAR (fonte proposta \u00b7 licen\u00e7a a conferir \u00b7 custo)\n\nCuradoria **offline, em tempo de commit** \u2014 o render segue determin\u00edstico e sem rede. Manifesto (fonte \u00b7\nURL \u00b7 data \u00b7 frase da licen\u00e7a) nasce **junto** com cada arquivo. **Nenhuma pe\u00e7a do spike `ada39fae`.**\n\n| Pe\u00e7a | Produ\u00e7\u00e3o proposta | Fonte 1380\u00d7720 | nossa pr\u00f3pria narra\u00e7\u00e3o | \u2014 (nossa) | `producedAt` + arquivo de \u00e1udio de origem | 0 |\n| `spotify_ui` | **Rota A** captura do NOSSO show no Spotify \u00b7 Rota CONFERIR** pelo Chrome do Sovereign (403 daqui) \u2014 frase no manifesto antes do render | `sourceRef` + `capturedAt` + `viewport` + m\u00e1scaras/crop declarados | 0 |\n| `capa_ep_cut` (\u00d72 exports) | capa 1:1 do epis\u00f3dio-testemunha + margem branca composta | arte PR\u00d3PRIA da casa | \u2014 | `producedAt` + epis\u00f3dio + `model:none, semiPx 0, stroke 25/18` | 0 |\n| `ep_video_card` | captura do NOSSO player no YouTube (mesmo epis\u00f3dio) + margem branca | YouTube (chrome em captura editorial) | **guideline A CONFERIR** (Chrome do Sovereign \u2014 YouTube bloqueia IP de datacenter) | `sourceRef` + `capturedAt` + `viewport` + `stroke 13` | 0 |\n| 4 `stamp` (+1 contingente) + `#kick` | texto no runner, Mont embutida | tipografia pr\u00f3pria (OFL, j\u00e1 no repo) | \u2014 | vers\u00e3o do runner (git) | 0 |\n| **degrau GATED** \u2014 logo Spotify vetorial isolado | \u2014 | **s\u00f3** brand kit oficial da Spotify | trademark nominativo + total do kit: 0 mco** \u2713 (alvo do Dramaturgo mantido \u2014 nenhuma rota paga, nenhuma gera\u00e7\u00e3o por IA).\n\n**\u26a0\ufe0f Depend\u00eancia de PRODU\u00c7\u00c3O (n\u00e3o \u00e9 arte, \u00e9 a\u00e7\u00e3o do Owner) \u2014 Rota A:** a coer\u00eancia do bloco depende de\no **mesmo epis\u00f3dio nosso** existir nas duas prateleiras. Se a casa n\u00e3o tiver show no Spotify (**A\nCONFERIR**), publicar o \u00e1udio de um epis\u00f3dio j\u00e1 no ar (Spotify for Creators \u00e9 gratuito) resolve as tr\u00eas\ncenas de uma vez: a prateleira \u00e9 nossa, a vaga \u00e9 real, a capa que pousa \u00e9 a nossa, e os g\u00eameos de 3C s\u00e3o\nverdadeiros. **Rota B degrada:** a capa que pousa passa a ser de terceiro e 3C ficaria com arte alheia\ncomo imagem final do bloco \u2014 **RECUSO** essa combina\u00e7\u00e3o; Rota B s\u00f3 sobrevive se 3C mantiver pe\u00e7a pr\u00f3pria,\nao custo de a prateleira e o g\u00eameo n\u00e3o serem o mesmo epis\u00f3dio (perda editorial declarada).\n\n### A3-8 \u00b7 LACUNAS (registro \u2014 nunca inven\u00e7\u00e3o)\n\n1. **Data de coleta de Libsyn/AdvertiseCast AUSENTE no roteiro.** Meu G4 pede `collectedAt`. **N\u00e3o\n   invento ano.** O `.ssrc` comporta a data (48 chars \u2248 611 px \u2264 620) \u2014 se a coleta vier, entra na tela.\n   N\u00e3o vindo: stamp com os nomes (ancorados) + lacuna **grep\u00e1vel no manifesto**\n   (`source_date_missing:libsyn,advertisecast`), e **eu n\u00e3o assino o carimbo como completo**. Decis\u00e3o do\n   Owner sobre publicar sem data.\n2. **Nenhuma lacuna num\u00e9rica nova.** Os 4 n\u00fameros do bloco (18\u201327 \u00b7 25.000 \u00b7 25 \u00b7 625) e o contingente\n   (2 bi+) s\u00e3o os do roteiro, **verbatim, uma vez cada** (G8 do Dramaturgo conferido pe\u00e7a a pe\u00e7a).\n3. **Guidelines de Spotify e YouTube n\u00e3o lidas por mim** \u2014 403 por IP de datacenter. Marcadas\n   A CONFERIR; **nenhuma afirma\u00e7\u00e3o sobre o que elas dizem** foi escrita aqui.\n4. **Larguras intr\u00ednsecas dos `stamp` s\u00e3o ESTIMATIVAS** por m\u00e9trica tipogr\u00e1fica (\u22480,60 em/d\u00edgito, caps\n   \u22480,68 em + tracking), n\u00e3o medi\u00e7\u00e3o de DOM \u2014 esta sess\u00e3o n\u00e3o roda render por diretiva. Todas viram\n   medi\u00e7\u00e3o no probe (A3-5.2).\n\n### A3-9 \u00b7 NOTAS DE CONTRATO (aos donos dos outros campos)\n\n1. **Ao Encenador \u2014 devolvo duas faixas de HUD.** `sourceStamp-esq` (3A) e `sourceStamp-dir` (3B/3C)\n   ficam **livres**: 3A n\u00e3o exibe n\u00famero (n\u00e3o h\u00e1 dado a carimbar) e em 3B/3C a proced\u00eancia viaja\n   **dentro** da pe\u00e7a (`stamp.ssrc`). Motivo material: o `#cap` do HUD \u00e9 multiplicado por `hudOut`\n   (`:481-488`) e morreria antes do dado nascer \u2014 a mesma armadilha que o bloco MACRO abriu como\n   VOC-ART-M-002. **Aqui ela n\u00e3o existe**, porque o v2 d\u00e1 vida pr\u00f3pria ao carimbo. Registro sem tocar no\n   arquivo deles.\n2. **Ao Encenador \u2014 duas caixas apertadas (envelope \u00d7 intr\u00ednseco).** `eq_fator_viz`: intr\u00ednseco \u2248302 vs\n   caixa 240 (+62, absorvido pelos v\u00e3os de 60/80 \u2014 sobram 29 e 49 px) \u00b7 `cpm_faixa_viz`: altura \u2248133 vs\n   130 (+3). N\u00e3o mudo n\u00famero dele; entrego a aritm\u00e9tica e o gate de probe.\n3. **Ao DoP \u2014 tr\u00eas corre\u00e7\u00f5es de or\u00e7amento e luz, todas a seu favor.** (a) `#wash`/`#weave` ficam **fora\n   do `#stage`** e **n\u00e3o recebem `dof`** (`:318-319`) \u21d2 o blur de 1,68 MP que ele or\u00e7ou em \u00a7F3-7 **n\u00e3o\n   acontece**: o bloco \u00e9 mais barato que o previsto, e o fundo permanece n\u00edtido (a separa\u00e7\u00e3o de\n   profundidade continua vindo do dof das folhas). (b) O `cutout` imp\u00f5e `drop-shadow(0 0 30px\n   rgba(0,215,255,.30))` (`:249`) \u2014 um halo ciano em toda pe\u00e7a recortada, que colide com o `rim: \"n-a\"`\n   que ele declarou. **Recomendo aceitar**: \u00e9 o glow da pe\u00e7a de refer\u00eancia APROVADA (fcf91d44) e incide\n   igual nos dois g\u00eameos de 3C, preservando a fotometria g\u00eamea; recus\u00e1-lo seria um pedido novo. (c) O\n   `selo_2x` executa pelo **fallback in-stage dele** \u2014 o HUD do v2 n\u00e3o tem slot central (A3-3).\n4. **Ao Dramaturgo \u2014 grafias que executam a delega\u00e7\u00e3o dele, sujeitas ao veto dele:** `SE 25.000`\n   (prefixo condicional que ele mesmo prop\u00f4s) \u00b7 `EXEMPLO NA FAIXA` no fator \u00b7 `POR EPIS\u00d3DIO \u00b7 CONTA\n   APROXIMADA` no resultado \u00b7 `\u2248` obrigat\u00f3rio. Nenhuma palavra de tese minha entrou em tela; o `#kick`\n   leva `\u00c1UDIO`, palavra do T\u00cdTULO do bloco no roteiro (G11 preservado por constru\u00e7\u00e3o).\n5. **A toda a trupe (rec\u00eancia vence):** no motor de produ\u00e7\u00e3o, **`counter` existe** (`:153-156`,\n   `:471-478`) \u21d2 o **PV-1** do Dramaturgo est\u00e1 materialmente atendido \u2014 com a ressalva de que ele sai\n   **sem numeral tabular** e vai tremer (VOC-ART-M-005). E `guardImages \u2192 __fatal` (`:503-518`) fecha o\n   buraco do `layerRef` \u00f3rf\u00e3o que o pr\u00f3prio ADENDO do meu mandato descrevia como aberto.\n\n---\n\n## NOTAS DE DISSENSO (Diretor de Arte \u2014 ressalvas; nada das lentes anteriores foi sobrescrito)\n\n- **R-A3-1 \u2014 `ep_video_card` deixa de ser l\u00e2mina e passa a ser recorte.** O Dramaturgo/Encenador o\n  especificaram como `screenshot` (\u21d2 kind `slab`). No v2, `slab` traz **borda violeta 1px + `.sheen` +\n  `.rim` + raio 32** (`:236-244`) enquanto o g\u00eameo `capa_ep_cut` \u00e9 um recorte com **contorno branco**.\n  Postos lado a lado, co-planares e \u00e0 mesma escala, seriam **duas esp\u00e9cies de objeto** \u2014 e a cena inteira\n  existe para dizer que s\u00e3o **o mesmo**. A gemelaridade que o Encenador construiu por aritm\u00e9tica e o DoP\n  selou por fotometria s\u00f3 fecha se a MAT\u00c9RIA tamb\u00e9m for g\u00eamea. **N\u00e3o mudo caixa, Z, beat ou tempo** \u2014\n  mudo a esp\u00e9cie de pe\u00e7a, que \u00e9 meu campo. Alternativa considerada e recusada: transformar a capa em\n  `slab` (perderia o pouso-adesivo na prateleira de 3A e a assinatura aprovada).\n- **R-A3-2 \u2014 o corpo tipogr\u00e1fico do motor \u00e9 FIXO e fica abaixo do piso do DoP.** Ele pediu (dissenso 4)\n  `cpm_faixa_viz` \u2265100 px e termos da equa\u00e7\u00e3o \u2265110 px *in-layer*. ## 🤖 Claude *(01:59:41)*

Arquivo estendido (657 → 1.100 linhas; nada apagado — as 3 lentes anteriores intactas):
**`/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/docs/roadmap/ep07-partituras/ato3-audio.md`** (seção `## ARTE (VOCAB-ART-MONTAR v1)`, a partir da linha 660)

**Resumo (5 linhas).** Escrevi a arte das 3 cenas contra o motor de PRODUÇÃO `scripts/motion/montar-template-v2.mjs` — sou a primeira lente a fazê-lo (as três anteriores escreveram contra `build-scene.mjs`), e conferi constante a constante que **toda a aritmética delas sobrevive** (perspective 1300 · drift 24/13 · dof k80/max11 · camZ por estações). 8 camadas mapeadas a kinds que EXECUTAM hoje: `waveform_viz` (cutout de alpha nativo gerado da nossa própria narração) · `spotify_ui` (slab — marca **só** como chrome de UI em captura editorial, `treatment: intacto`, guideline A CONFERIR pelo Chrome do Sovereign, 403 daqui) · `capa_ep_cut`/`ep_video_card` (cutouts com **sticker-white geométrico**, `model: none`, franja ZERO — este bloco não roda u2net em peça nenhuma) · 4 `stamp` para a conta. A honestidade do dado vive **dentro** da peça: `US$ 18 – 27` grafado como intervalo (sem média inventada), `SE 25.000 · HIPÓTESE`, `× US$ 25 · EXEMPLO NA FAIXA`, `≈ US$ 625 · POR EPISÓDIO · CONTA APROXIMADA`, e `LIBSYN · ADVERTISECAST` no `.ssrc` da própria peça do CPM — porque o `#cap` do HUD morre no `hudOut` (`:481-488`). Peso ótico fechado por aritmética: **spread 2,7% ≤ 10%** entre os gêmeos de 3C, com dois exports da mesma capa justificados (o caminho ingênuo daria 32%).

**Achados materiais que valem para a trupe inteira:** o `stamp` do v2 carrega linha de fonte com vida própria (resolve na prática o **VOC-ART-004** e dá caminho nativo ao VOC-ART-M-002 do bloco MACRO) · `guardImages → __fatal` (`:503-518`) **fecha** o G-ART-MONTAR-6 · o kind `counter` existe (**PV-1 do Dramaturgo materialmente atendido**) mas **sem numeral tabular** (grep = 0) · `cutout` impõe glow ciano em toda peça (`:249`) ⇒ marca de terceiro **nunca** entra como cutout · **ancoragem por kind**: `cutout/stamp/chip/badge/counter` ancoram pelo canto superior-esquerdo, e todas as coordenadas das lentes anteriores são centros (conversão obrigatória antes do render).

**Dissensos registrados (sem sobrescrever ninguém):** R-A3-1 `ep_video_card` vira recorte (dois materiais matariam a gemelaridade) · R-A3-2 o corpo `.sv` é 46 px fixo, abaixo do piso do DoP · R-A3-3 a caixa de HUD do `selo_2x` não é materializável no v2 (assino o fallback in-stage dele).

**Pedidos de Vocabulário (2):**
- **VOC-ART-M-004** — `slab.plain` (lâmina sem sheen/rim/borda/opacity .95): protege marca de terceiro do vestuário do container (G2) no degrau GATED do logo isolado.
- **VOC-ART-M-005** — `style` por camada nos kinds de texto (`size` · `color` · `numerals: tabular`): destrava o piso tipográfico do DoP, o token da casa "gold só para VALOR" (hoje **não executa** — afeta ato1 e macro também) e o contador sem tremor.

**Lacunas declaradas, não inventadas:** a data de coleta de Libsyn/AdvertiseCast não existe no roteiro (`source_date_missing:libsyn,advertisecast` — não assino o carimbo como completo sem ela) e uma dependência de **produção** para o Owner: publicar o áudio de um episódio nosso no Spotify (grátis) torna prateleira, vaga, capa e gêmeos todos nossos — sem isso, a Rota B degrada e eu recuso arte de terceiro como imagem final do bloco.
